| Booking Abandonment | Recover abandoned flight searches and incomplete bookings through multi-channel price alert campaigns. System detects when a customer searches for a route, adds flights to cart, or starts booking process but doesn't complete. Triggers personalized recovery sequence with dynamic pricing updates, seat availability warnings, and time-sensitive offers based on route popularity and customer's search history. | High booking abandonment rate causing significant revenue leakage. Customers leave during payment process or after seeing total price. No systematic follow-up mechanism to recover these high-intent prospects. | Still looking at Istanbul-Paris for March 15? Great news - prices just dropped 15%! Only 4 seats left at this fare. Your selected Economy Flex fare is now $489 (was $575). Complete your booking in the next 2 hours and get free seat selection. [Book Now Button] | Booking Recovery Rate (target: 12-18%), Revenue Recovered from Abandonment, Cart-to-Purchase Conversion, Time-to-Recovery | Trigger: Cart abandonment event or search without booking within 30 min. Include dynamic pricing API integration for real-time fare updates. A/B test recovery timing (1hr vs 4hr vs 24hr). | M1, M3, M4, M7, M10, M15 |
